## Inference Research Guidance Check

- IDA fact, documentation evidence, source fact, and inference are separated throughout.

- Historical documentation assertions were treated as hypotheses. The 123-message claim and 121-pointer claims in support docs were disproved by source enumeration and pointer-table length.
